CORPORATE STRUCTURE AND CULTURE
企業架構與文化

Midea Real Estate Holding Limited (the “Company” and together with its        美 的 置 業 控 股 有 限 公 司（「本 公 司」）及 其 附 屬
subsidiaries, the “Group” or “Midea Real Estate”) (Stock Code: 3990.HK) is    公 司（「本 集 團」）或（「美 的 置 業」） （股 份 代 號：
a listed company of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock       3990.HK），是香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）
Exchange”). The Group is based in the PRC and operates the following major    上市企業。本集團以中國為總部，並經營以下主
businesses:                                                                   要業務：

 MILESTONES
 大事記
MAR
3月
        Asset operation: Industrial park secured
        first third-party operation project
        — Shunbei • Ruichuang Intelligent
        Manufacturing Park, achieving a new
        breakthrough in light-asset model
        資產運營：產業園首拓順北 • 睿創智造園
        第三方運營項目，實現輕資產模式新突破
        We won the bid for Shunbei • Ruichuang Intelligent
        Manufacturing Park, taking the lead in agency investment promotion and operational management, further expanding its
        industrial park operation footprint.
        中標順北 • 睿創智造園，主導代理招商及代運營管理，再拓產業園區運營版圖。


JUN
6月
        Project management services: The inaugural representative work
        of project management services, exceeding expectations in both
        volume and price
        開發服務：首個開發服務運營代表作，量價雙超預期
        The first launch of Wuxi Midea Dongwangfu was a success, achieving sales of RMB472 million
        with a sell-out rate over 90%. Its single-project sales volume surpassed the total annual sales
        of high-end improved residences in the entire Wuxi East sector.
        無錫美的東望府首開告捷，斬獲人民幣 4.72 億元，去化率超 90%，單盤銷量力壓無
        錫東板塊全年高端改善住宅總量。




JUN
6月
        Property management services: Honored as“2025 China
        Property Service Enterprises Comprehensive Strength
        TOP16”
        物管服務：榮膺「2025 中國物業服務企業綜合實力 TOP16」
        Midea Real Estate Services, with its core philosophy “Using Certainty to Forge
        Excellent Service,” expressed its thoughts, understanding, and practice regarding new
        quality service capabilities and the transformation and upgrading of the property
        management industry.
        美置服務以「用確定性，鑄就好服務」這一核心觀點，表達了新質服務力以及
        物管行業轉型升級的思考、理解與踐行。




 8    MIDEA REAL ESTATE HOLDING LIMITED       2025 ANNUAL REPORT
                                                                                             Milestones (Continued)
                                                                                                        大事記（續）

JUL
7月
      Asset operation: Launched“Beijiao Vitality
      Island,”a new community commercial
      project, initiating a new commercial
      operation model
      資產運營：啟幕「北滘活力島」新型社區商
      業，開啟商業運營新模式
      Breaking away from the single function of traditional sports
      centers or shopping malls, it integrates nature, commerce,
      culture, and community life to create an open social space with both vibrancy and a sense of relaxation.
      打破傳統體育中心或商場的單一功能，融合自然、商業、文化與社區生活，打造一個兼具煙火氣與鬆弛
      感的開放式社交空間。


AUG
8月
      Asset operation: Entered Guangzhou, developing its first
      Yueran Series commercial complex
      資產運營：落子廣州，打造首個悅然系商業綜合體
      This project, jointly developed by Midea Real Estate Commercial and Guangzhou
      Kehong, is located in the Huangpu West District of Guangzhou, comprising a 120,000
      ㎡ commercial complex.
      該項目由美置商業與廣州科鴻聯合打造，落子廣州黃埔西區打造 12 萬㎡商業
      綜合體。




DEC
12月
      Real estate technology: Remac TY Won
      the 2025 WDA World Design Awards
      — Winner Award
      房地產科技：睿住天元斬獲 2025 WDA 世界
      設計大獎 — Winner 獎
      Received the 2025 WDA World Design Awards — Winner Award,
      gaining international recognition for its project design standards and implementation quality.
      摘得 2025 WDA 世界設計大獎 — Winner 獎，項目設計水準與落地品質獲國際認可。

      PRODUCT STRENGTH 產品力
     Remac TY design won three awards at the Fifth “New
     Infrastructure Cup” Intelligent Construction and BIM
     Application Competition
     睿住天元設計榮獲第五屆「新基建杯」智能建造及 BIM
     應用大賽三大獎項

     Remac TY project “Midea • Junlan Jinshou” was
     awarded “2025 Hangzhou Garden Greenery
     Five-Star Demonstration Project”
     睿住天元項目「美的 • 君蘭錦繡」榮獲「2025 年杭州市
     園林綠化五星示範項目」

     Remac TY design won three awards at the Guangdong
     Provincial Second “Beautiful Guangdong Countryside”
     Landscape Design Competition

I.    Industry Overview                                                                 一、行業概述
      In 2025, with gradual emergence from cyclical bottom fluctuations,                   2025 年，中 國 房 地 產 行 業 逐 步 走 出 週 期 底
      China’s real estate sector has entered a new phase characterized by                  部 震 盪，邁 入「存 量 提 質、多 元 共 生」新 階
      “improving the quality and efficiency of existing assets and advocating              段，政 策 持 續 發 力，「好 房 子」理 念 首 次 寫
      diversity and mutual promotion”. Policy support continued. This was                  入 政 府 工 作 報 告，成 為 行 業 發 展 的 核 心 導
      the first time the concept of “good house” was included in China’s                   向。市 場 呈 現 企 穩 回 暖、分 化 加 劇 態 勢，
      government work report, becoming a core guiding principle for industry               核 心 城 市 優 質 資 產 價 值 凸 顯，三 四 線 城 市
      development. The market showed signs of stabilization and recovery                   仍處於去化調整期，行業競爭已轉向品質、
      amid increasing polarisation. Quality assets in core cities rose in value,           服務與運營能力的綜合較量。
      while third- and fourth-tier cities were still in a phase of inventory
      adjustment. Industry competition has shifted toward a comprehensive
      contest of quality, service and operational capabilities.

      As the industry deepens its transition from “incremental development”                隨著行業從「增量開發」向「存量運營」轉型
      to “stock operation”, only enterprises with a focus on building “                    深 化，聚 焦「好 房 子」打 造、深 耕 服 務 品 質
      good house”, deepening its commitment toward service quality                         與 運 營 能 力 且 財 務 結 構 穩 健 的 企 業，方 能
      and operational capabilities, and sound financial structures can seize               在行業重構中搶佔先機、實現高質量發展。
      opportunities in the industry’s restructuring and achieve high-quality
      development.

      from “business restructuring” to “ quality and efficiency enhancement”.              越。

      while optimizing asset structure continuously. “Based on high-quality                優 化 資 產 結 構，以「優 質 持 有 型 資 產 為 根

IV. Future Prospect                                                                  四、未來展望
     Currently, the real estate industry is still in a transitional stage, yet the    當 前，房 地 產 行 業 仍 處 於 轉 型 階 段，但 曙
     dawn is approaching. The pivotal position of the “good houses” idea is           光 已 經 越 來 越 近，「好 房 子」理 念 的 核 心 地
     increasingly prominent. Returning to customer-oriented mindset and               位 不 斷 凸 顯，回 歸 客 戶 思 維、回 歸 價 值 創
     value creation will create new space for corporate development.                  造，將為企業發展開闢新空間。

     With a focus on asset-light businesses and support by three major                聚 焦 輕 資 產 業 務，以 產 品、服 務、運 營 三
     capabilities: product, service, and operations, we continuously enhance          大 能 力 為 支 撐，持 續 強 化 核 心 競 爭 力，是
     our core competitiveness, which is fundamental to sustainable                    本 集 團 可 持 續 發 展 的 根 本；依 託 優 質 持 有

Principal Risks and Uncertainties                                                  主要風險及不明朗因素
Principal risks and uncertainties of the Group include:                            本集團的主要風險及不明朗因素包括：

has considerable experience in the business operation of the Group, the Board        生於本集團業務營運方面擁有豐富的經驗，董事

facilitates the execution of the Group’s strategic aims and accelerates business     執行本集團的戰略目標及加快推動經營發展。董
development. The Board also believes that this structure would not impair the        事會亦認為，此架構不會打破董事會與本公司管
balance of power and authority between the Board and the management of               理層之間的權力及權限平衡，此乃因為董事會由
the Company because the Board comprises nine experienced and high-calibre            九名經驗豐富且具有良好誠信的高素質人才組成，

Board Diversity Policy                                                               董事會成員多元化政策
The Board has adopted a board diversity policy with effect from 12 September         董事會已採納自 2018 年 9 月 12 日起生效的董事會
2018 which sets out the approach to achieve diversity on the Board.                  成員多元化政策，當中載列達致董事會成員多元
                                                                                     化的方法。

Selection of candidates will be based on a range of diversity perspectives,          甄選候選人將按一系列多元化範疇為基準，包括
including but not limited to gender, age, cultural and educational background,       但不限於性別、年齡、文化及教育背景、種族、

Company Secretary                                                                  公司秘書
The Company Secretary plays an important role in supporting the Board by           公司秘書擔任協助董事會的重要角色，以確保董
ensuring good information flow within the Board and that the Board policies        事會內信息暢通以及董事會政策及程序獲遵守。
and procedures are duly followed. The Company Secretary reports to the Board       公司秘書透過聯席主席兼總裁向董事會匯報企業
through the Co-Chairman and President on corporate governance matters              管治事務，而所有董事均可向公司秘書取得建議
while all Directors have access to the advice and services of the Company          及服務。

RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL CONTROL
風險管理及內部監控

The Group’s businesses, financial condition, results of operation and growth        本集團的業務、財務狀況、經營業績及增長前景
prospects may be affected by risks and uncertainties directly or indirectly.        可能會直接或間接受到風險及不確定因素的影響。
The Board acknowledges its responsibility for maintaining effective risk            董事會確認其負責維護本集團有效的風險管理及
management and internal control systems of the Group and reviewing the              內部監控系統及檢討該等系統的有效性。本公司
effectiveness of these systems. The Company adopts risk management and              採納風險管理及內部監控系統以加強與管理層的
internal control systems to enhance the accountability and communication            問責及與其交流已識別風險、評估已識別風險的
of identified risks with management, measure the impact of the identified           影響及積極促進實施減低風險的措施。該等系統
risks and facilitate implementation of mitigation measures proactively. These       旨 在 提 供 合 理（而 非 絕 對）保 證 並 無 重 大 失 實 陳
systems are designed to provide reasonable, but not absolute, assurance             述或損失，以及管理（而非消除）未能達成本集團
against material misstatement or loss, and to manage, but not eliminate, risks      目標的風險。
of failure in achieving the Group’s objective.

Risk Management and Internal Control Systems                                        風險管理及內部監控系統
The Group’s risk management and internal control systems include a defined          本集團的風險管理及內部監控系統包括附有指定
structure with specified limits of authority and responsibility. Each of the        權限及責任的清晰架構。各業務單位作為風險預
business departments acts as the primary unit of risk prevention and control,       防和控制的主責單位，負責日常風險管理及內部
responsible for the implementation of daily risk management and internal            監控的實施、內外部風險資訊的識別與收集並協
control, identification and collection of internal and external risk information    助內部審計職能進行定期風險評估。管理層負責

Risk Management Approach                                                              風險管理方針
Our approach to risk management is to identify current risk exposures and             我們的風險管理方針是識別現時風險承擔並隨後
manage them so that they can be understood, controlled or mitigated. All              對其進行監管，藉以瞭解、控制或減低有關風險。

strategy and business objectives. They also aim to identify, assess, evaluate and     標的重大風險，亦致力於識別、評估、檢討及減
mitigate operational risks. Many aspects of risks are considered, including but       低營運風險。各方面的風險均被納入考慮，包括
not limited to business continuity, financial impact, reputational risk, safety and   但不限於業務持續性、財務影響、聲譽風險、安
health, external regulations (including the Listing Rules) and environmental,         全及健康、外部規例（包括上市規則）及與環境、
social and governance related risks.                                                  社會及管治相關的風險。

      3.2 Capital management                                                             3.2 資本管理
          The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard                         本集團管理資本旨在保障本集團持續
          the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order to                        經營的能力，藉以回報擁有人及使其
          provide returns for the owner and benefits for other stakeholders                     他利益相關者受益，同時維持最佳資
          and to maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the cost of                    本構架以減少資本成本。
          capital.

          In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group                       為維持或調整資本結構，本集團可調
          may adjust the amount of dividends paid to the owner, issue new                       整派付予擁有人的股息款額、發行新
          shares or sell assets to reduce debt.                                                 股份或出售資產以降低債務。

     3.3 Fair value estimation                                                            3.3 公平值估計
         The table below analyses the Group’s financial instruments carried                 下表根據在評估公平值的估值技術中
         at fair value as at 31 December 2025 and 2024, by level of the                     所運用的輸入數據分級，分析本集團
         inputs to valuation techniques used to measure fair value. Such                    於 2025 年 及 2024 年 12 月 31 日 按 公 平
         inputs are categorised into three levels within a fair value hierarchy             值入賬的金融工具。有關輸入數據按
         as follows:                                                                        公平值等級分為如下三個等級：

         •     Level 1: The fair value of financial instruments traded in                   •   第 1 級：於活躍市場買賣的金融
               active markets (such as publicly traded derivatives, and                         工具（如公開買賣的衍生工具以
               trading and available-for-sale securities) is based on quoted                    及買賣及可供出售證券）的公平
               market prices at the end of each of the reporting periods.                       值乃根據各報告期末的市場報
               These instruments are included in level 1.                                       價計算。該等工具計入第 1 級。

         •     Level 2: The fair value of financial instruments that are not                •   第 2 級：未在活躍市場上交易的
               traded in an active market (for example, over-the-counter                        金 融 工 具（例 如 場 外 衍 生 工 具）
               derivatives) is determined using valuation techniques which                      的 公 平 值 採 用 估 值 技 術 釐 定，

            Upon entering into a contract with a customer, the Group obtains                 在與客戶簽訂合約時，本集團獲得從
            rights to receive consideration from the customer and assumes                    客戶處收取代價的權利，並承擔向客
            performance obligations to transfer goods or provide services to                 戶轉讓貨物或提供服務的履約義務。
            the customer. The combination of those rights and performance                    根據剩餘權利和履約義務之間的關係，
            obligations gives rise to a net asset or a net liability depending               這些權利和履約義務的組合會產生淨
            on the relationship between the remaining rights and the                         資產或淨負債。如果剩餘權利的計量
            performance obligations. The contract is an asset and recognised                 超過剩餘履約義務的計量，則合約為
            as contract assets if the measure of the remaining rights                        資產並確認為合約資產。反之，如果
            exceeds the measure of the remaining performance obligations.                    剩餘履約義務的計量超過剩餘權利的
            Conversely, the contract is a liability and recognised as contract               計量，則合約為負債，並確認為合約
            liabilities if the measure of the remaining performance obligations              負債。
            exceeds the measure of the remaining rights.

                  product procurement, revenue is recognized based on the                           及 產 品，故 收 入 乃 根 據 本 集 團

                  selling price of the goods paid by customers.                                     支付之貨品銷售總價確認。

                  For the installation service, the revenue is recognized over                      就 安 裝 服 務 而 言，收 入 乃 根 據
                  time based on the progress towards complete satisfaction                          完全履行履約義務的進度分期
                  of a performance obligation. At each reporting period end,                        確認。在每個報告期末，本集團
                  the Group re-evaluates the stage of completion, which is                          重 新 評 估 完 成 階 段，主 要 採 用
                  mainly measured using the output method to reflect the                            產 出 法 計 量，以 反 映 合 約 履 行

16. Investment properties (Continued)                                              16. 投資物業（續）
      Valuation processes of the Group                                               本集團的估值程序
      A valuation of the Group’s investment properties as at 31 December             本集團於 2025 年及 2024 年 12 月 31 日的投資
      2025 and 2024 was carried out by the management or an independent              物業由管理層及持有相關認可專業資格的
      and professionally qualified valuer, Jones Lang LaSalle, who holds a           獨立及專業合資格估值師仲量聯行進行估
      recognised relevant professional qualification and has recent experience       值，有 關 估 值 師 近 期 對 所 估 值 投 資 物 業 的
      in the locations and nature of the investment properties being valued.         位置及性質有經驗。就所有投資物業而言，
      For all investment properties, their current use equates the highest and       彼等現有用途即最高及最佳用途。
      best use.

      Discussions of valuation processes and results were held between               管理層及估值師定期討論本集團中期及年
      management and the valuer on a regular basis, in line with the Group’s         度報告日期的估值程序及結果。
      the interim and annual reporting dates.

      At each financial year end, management:                                        於各財政年度末，管理層將：

      •     Verifies all major inputs to the independent valuation report;           •   核實獨立估值報告的所有重大輸入數
                                                                                         據；

      •     Assesses property valuation movements when compared to the               •   評估物業估值與上年度估值報告相比
            prior year valuation report; and                                             的變動；及

      •     Holds discussions with the independent valuer.                           •   與獨立估值師討論。

      Valuation techniques                                                           估值方法
      Valuations are based on:                                                       估值乃按以下方法進行：

      Income approach taking into account the current rents of the property          使 用 收 入 法，計 及 物 業 權 益 的 現 時 租 金 及
      interests and the reversionary potentials of the tenancies, term yield and     重 訂 租 約 的 可 能，隨 後 分 別 以 租 期 收 益 率
      reversionary yield are then applied respectively to derive the market          及復歸收益率計算物業的市值。
      value of the property.

      There were no changes to the valuation techniques during the year.             年內的估值方法並無變動。

      Relationships of unobservable inputs to fair value are as follows:               不可觀察輸入數據與公平值的關係如下：

      •     The higher rate of return/capitalisation rate, the lower fair value;       •   回報率╱資本化比率越高，公平值越
                                                                                           低；

      •     The higher monthly rental, the higher fair value.                          •   月租越高，公平值越高。

      •     The higher expected vacancy rate, the lower fair value;                    •   預期空置率越高，公平值越低；

      Leasing arrangements                                                             租賃安排
      The investment properties are leased to tenants under operating leases           投 資 物 業 根 據 經 營 租 賃 出 租 予 租 戶，按 月
      with rentals payable monthly. Where considered necessary to reduce               支 付 租 金。倘 認 為 有 必 要 降 低 信 用 風 險，
      credit risk, the Group may obtain rental deposits for the term of the lease.     本集團可在租賃期內獲得租金按金。

      Although the Group is exposed to changes in the residual value at the            儘管本集團在當前租賃期末面臨殘值變動
      end of the current leases, the Group typically enters into new operating         的 風 險，本 集 團 通 常 會 訂 立 新 經 營 租 賃，
      leases and therefore will not immediately realise any reduction in               因此於租賃期末不會立即變現任何殘值削
      residual value at the end of these leases. Expectations about the future         減。對 未 來 殘 值 的 預 期 反 映 於 物 業 的 公 平
      residual values are reflected in the fair value of the properties.               值內。

              Trade receivables are amounts due from customers for properties                  貿易應收款項指於日常業務過程中就

                                                                                               款項分類為流動資產，否則呈列為非
                                                                                               流動資產。

              Trade and other receivables are recognised initially at the amount               貿易及其他應收款項按公平值確認時，
              of consideration that is unconditional unless they contain                       初步會以無條件對價金額確認，除非
              significant financing components, when they are recognised at fair               其包含重大融資組成部分。本集團持
              value. The Group holds the trade receivables with the objective                  有貿易應收款項，旨在收回合約現金
              of collecting the contractual cash flows and therefore measures                  流量，因此其後利用實際利率法按攤
              them subsequently at amortised cost using the effective interest                 銷成本計量貿易應收款項。有關本集
              method. See note 3.1 for a description of the group’s impairment                 團減值政策的說明，請參閱附註 3.1。
              policies.

26. Share-based payment                                                             26. 以股份為基礎的付款
      On 22 April 2021, the Company granted a total of 66,660,000 share               於 2021 年 4 月 22 日，本 公 司 向 193 位 合 資 格
      options to 193 eligible participants, who are certain directors, senior         參與者（為本集團若干董事、高級管理層及
      management and employees of the Group. The share options shall                  僱 員）授 出 合 共 66,660,000 份 購 股 權。該 等
      entitle the eligible participants to subscribe for a total of 66,660,000        購股權將賦予合資格參與者權利以認購本
      ordinary shares of the Company. Pursuant to the terms of the share              公 司 合 共 66,660,000 股 普 通 股。根 據 購 股 權
      option scheme, the options granted are subject to certain performance           計 劃 的 條 款，所 授 出 的 購 股 權 須 受 限 於 若
      conditions.                                                                     干表現條件。

      on the following rates for the relevant financial year, provided that the       政 年 度 按 下 列 比 率 歸 屬，惟 須 待 上 述 歸 屬
      vesting conditions above are satisfied: (i) 40% of the total number of          條件達成後方可作實：(i) 購股權總數的 40%

      number of the share options will be vested 22 April 2024; and (iii) 20%         的 40% 將 於 2024 年 4 月 22 日 歸 屬；及 (iii) 購
      of the total number of the share options will be vested 22 April 2025. If       股權總數的 20% 將於 2025 年 4 月 22 日歸屬。
      the vesting conditions above have not been fulfilled, the corresponding         倘 上 述 歸 屬 條 件 未 能 達 成，則 所 授 出 購 股
      percentage of the share options granted will lapse.                             權的相應百分比將失效。

28. Other financial liabilities under supplier finance                             28. 供應商融資安排下其他金融負債
    arrangement
      During the year ended 31 December 2025, the Group entered into                 截 至 2025 年 12 月 31 日 止 年 度，本 集 團 訂 立
      supplier finance arrangements to support several suppliers with their          供 應 商 融 資 安 排，以 支 援 數 家 供 應 商 的 現
      cash flows. Under these arrangements, banks acquired the rights to             金 流。根 據 該 等 安 排，銀 行 取 得 供 應 商 選
      selected trade receivables from the suppliers. The terms and conditions        定 貿 易 應 收 款 項 的 權 利。安 排 的 條 款 及 條
      of the arrangement are unchanged from the original trade payables from         件與該供應商的初始貿易應付款項並無不
      this supplier, except that the Group received extended payment terms           同，惟 本 集 團 獲 得 了 更 長 的 付 款 期 限，或
      or the finance providers have received more surety from the Group than         融資供應商從本集團獲得的擔保程度高於
      the original suppliers would have received. This indicated that the nature     原 供 應 商 所 能 獲 得 的 擔 保，這 表 明 負 債 性
      of the liability has changed. Consequently, the original trade payables        質 已 發 生 變 化。因 此，原 貿 易 應 付 款 項 應
      should be derecognised and reclassified as “other financial liabilities        予以終止確認，並重新分類為「供應商融資
      under supplier finance arrangements”.                                          安排下其他金融負債」。

      The repayment terms of the other financial liabilities under supplier          供應商融資安排下其他金融負債的還款條

        Investments in subsidiaries are accounted for at cost less               於附屬公司的投資按成本扣除減值列
        impairment. Cost includes direct attributable costs of investment.       賬。成本包括投資直接應佔成本。附

        the period the dividend is declared or if the carrying amount of         超出合併財務報表所示被投資公司資
        the investment in the separate financial statements exceeds the          產淨值（包括商譽）之賬面值，則須對
        carrying amount in the consolidated financial statements of the          附屬公司之投資進行減值測試。
        investee’s net assets including goodwill.

      39.4 Segment reporting                                                         39.4 分部報告
          Operating segments are reported in a manner consistent with                   經營分部按照向首席經營決策者提供
          the internal reporting provided to the chief operating decision               的內部報告貫徹一致的方式報告。作
          maker. The chief operating decision-maker, who is responsible for             出策略性決定的首席經營決策者被認
          allocating resources and assessing performance of the operating               定為執行董事，負責分配資源和評估
          segments, has been identified as the executive directors who                  經營分部的表現。
          make strategic decisions.

          loss.                                                                      損益。

    39.9 Impairment of non-financial assets                                       39.9 非金融資產減值

         life are not subject to amortisation and are tested annually               毋須攤銷，惟須每年進行減值測試，

         recoverable. An impairment loss is recognised for the amount               按資產賬面值超出可收回金額的金額
         by which the asset’s carrying amount exceeds its recoverable               確認。可收回金額指資產公平值減出
         amount. The recoverable amount is the higher of an asset’s fair            售成本及使用價值的較高者。就評估
         value less costs of disposal and value in use. For the purposes of         減值而言，資產按獨立可識別現金流

         are largely independent of the cash inflows from other assets              現金流入很大程度上獨立開來。於各
         or groups of assets (cash-generating units). Non-financial assets          報告期末審閱已減值的非金融資產（商
         other than goodwill that suffered an impairment are reviewed for           譽除外）是否可能撥回減值。
         possible reversal of the impairment at the end of each reporting
         period.

      39.11 Financial assets                                                          39.11 金融資產

                 The Group classifies its financial assets in the following                    本集團將金融資產按以下計量

                 •      those to be measured subsequently at fair value                        •   隨 後 按 公 平 值（計 入 其 他
                        (either through other comprehensive income, or                             綜 合 收 益 或 計 入 損 益）計
                        through profit or loss), and                                               量的金融資產；及

                 •      those to be measured at amortised cost.                                •   按攤銷成本計量的金融資

    39.12 Offsetting financial instruments                                             39.12 抵銷金融工具
         Financial assets and liabilities are offset and the net amount                   當有法定可執行權利可抵銷已確認金
         reported in the consolidated balance sheet when there is a legally               額，並有意圖按淨額基準結清或同時
         enforceable right to offset the recognised amounts and there is                  變現資產及結清負債時，有關金融資
         an intention to settle on a net basis or realise the asset and settle            產與負債可互相抵銷，並於合併資產
         the liability simultaneously. The legally enforceable right must not             負債表列報淨額。法定可執行權利不
         be contingent on future events and must be enforceable in the                    依賴未來事件而定，可於一般業務過
         normal course of business and in the event of default, insolvency                程中以及本公司或對手方出現違約、
         or bankruptcy of the Company or the counterparty.                                無力償債或破產時強制執行。

         The Group assesses on a forward-looking basis the expected credit                本集團按前瞻性基準評估與按攤銷成
         losses associated with its assets carried at amortised cost and debt             本計量的資產及按公平值計入其他綜

         Group determines whether there has been a significant increase                   如何確定信用風險有否大幅增加。預
         in credit risk. Expected credit losses are a probability-weighted                期信貸虧損為於金融資產預期存續期
         estimate of credit losses (i.e. the present value of all cash shortfalls)        內對 信貸 虧損的 概率加權估 計（即所
         over the expected life of the financial assets.                                  有現金短缺額的現值）。

           analysed.                                                                     動。

    39.15 Properties under development and Completed                             39.15 在建物業及已竣工待售物業

      39.16 Contract assets, contract acquisition costs and                         39.16 合約資產、取得合約的成本與合
            contract liabilities                                                          約負債
           Upon entering into a contract with a customer, the Group obtains            與客戶簽訂合約時，本集團有權向客
           rights to receive consideration from the customer and assumes               戶收取對價，並承擔向客戶轉移貨物
           performance obligations to transfer goods or services to the                或服務的履約責任。該等權利與履約
           customer. The combination of those rights and performance                   責任的結合會產生淨資產或淨負債，
           obligations gives rise to a net asset or a net liability depending          取決於剩餘權利與履約責任的關係。
           on the relationship between the remaining rights and the                    倘收取對價的剩餘有條件權利的計量
           performance obligations. The contract is an asset and recognised            超過所履行的履約責任，則合約為資
           as contract assets if the measure of the remaining conditional              產並確認為合約資產。反之，倘餘下
           rights to consideration exceeds the satisfied performance                   履約責任的計量超過餘下權利，則合
           obligations. Conversely, the contract is a liability and recognised         約為負債並確認為合約負債。
           as contract liabilities if the measure of the remaining performance
           obligations exceeds the measure of the remaining rights.

           The Group recognises the incremental costs of obtaining a                   本集團在預期能收回自客戶取得合約
           contract with a customer as contract acquisition cost within                所增加成本的情況下於合約資產內確
           contract assets if the Group expects to recover these costs.                認有關成本為取得合約的成本。

           investments with original maturities of three months or less that           隨時可轉換為確定金額現金及價值變
           are readily convertible to known amounts of cash and which are              動風險不重大的的其他短期高流動性
           subject to an insignificant risk of changes in value. Bank deposits         投資。限制使用的銀行存款計入合併
           which are restricted to use are included in “restricted cash” of the        資產負債表「受限制現金」。受限制現
           consolidated balance sheet. Restricted cash are excluded from               金不計入現金及現金等價物。
           cash and cash equivalents.

    39.18 Share capital                                                             39.18 股本
         Ordinary shares are classified as equity.                                      普通股分類為權益。

         Incremental costs directly attributable to the issue of new shares             發行新股份直接產生的新增成本於權
         are shown in equity as a deduction, net of tax, from the proceeds.             益列示為自所得款項扣減（扣除稅項）。

         Where any group company purchases the Company’s equity share                   倘任何集團公司購買本公司的權益股
         capital, the consideration paid, including any directly attributable           本，則 所 付 對 價（包 括 直 接 應 佔 增 量
         incremental costs (net of income taxes) is deducted from equity                成本（扣除所得稅））自本公司擁有人
         attributable to owners of the Company as treasury shares until                 應佔權益扣除作庫存股份，直至有關
         the shares are cancelled or reissued. Where such ordinary shares               股份被註銷或獲發行為止。倘隨後再
         are subsequently reissued, any consideration received (net of                  發 行 有 關 普 通 股，任 何 已 收 對 價（扣
         any directly attributable incremental transaction costs and the                除任何直接應佔新增交易成本及相關
         related income tax effects) is included in equity attributable to the          所得 稅影 響）計入本公司 擁有人應佔
         Company’s owners.                                                              權益。

         Shares held for the restricted share award scheme are disclosed as             為受限制股份獎勵計劃而持有的股份
         treasury shares and deducted from contributed equity.                          作為庫存股披露，並自注資權益扣減。

    39.19 Government grants                                                         39.19 政府補貼
         Grants from the government are recognised at their fair value                  政府補貼在能夠合理保證將會收到補
         where there is a reasonable assurance that the grant will be                   貼且本集團符合所有附帶條件時按公
         received and the Group will comply with all attached conditions.               平值確認。

         Government grants relating to costs are deferred and recognised                與成本相關的政府補貼會遞延至須與
         in the profit or loss over the period necessary to match them with             擬補貼的成本相應入賬的期間於損益
         the costs that they are intended to compensate. For government                 確認。與收益有關的政府補助，倘有
         grants related to income, where the grant is a compensation                    關補助用於補償以後期間發生的相關
         for related expenses or losses to be incurred in the subsequent                開支或虧損，有關補助確認為遞延收
         periods, the grant is recognised as deferred income, and                       益，並於相關成本發生期間的損益確
         recognised in the profit or loss over the periods in which the                 認；倘有關補助用於補償本集團已經
         related costs are incurred; where the grant is a compensation                  發生的相關開支或虧損，有關補助於
         for related expenses or losses already incurred by the Group, the              損益確認。
         grant is recognised in the profit or loss.

         Government grants relating to the purchase of assets are included              與購買資產相關的政府補貼作為遞延
         in non-current liabilities as deferred income and are credited to              收入計入非流動負債，並於有關資產
         profit or loss on a straight-line basis over the expected lives of the         的預計年限按直線基準計入損益。
         related assets.

      39.20 Trade and other payables                                                 39.20 貿易及其他應付款項
          These amounts represent liabilities for goods and services                     該等款項指財政年度結束前向本集團
          provided to the Group prior to the end of financial year which                 提供貨品及服務而未獲支付的負債。
          are unpaid. Trade and other payables are presented as current                  貿易及其他應付款項呈列為流動負債，
          liabilities, unless payment is not due within 12 months after the              除非付款並非在報告期間後 12 個月內
          reporting period.                                                              到期。

          Trade and other payables are recognised initially at their fair value          貿易及其他應付款項初步按公平值確
          and subsequently measured at amortised cost using the effective                認，其後採用實際利率法按攤銷成本
          interest method.                                                               計量。

      39.21 Borrowings                                                               39.21 借款
          Borrowings are recognised initially at fair value, net of transaction          借款初步按公平值扣除交易成本確認，
          costs incurred. Borrowings are subsequently carried at amortised               其 後 按 攤 銷 成 本 列 賬，所 得 款 項（扣
          cost; any difference between the proceeds (net of transaction                  除交 易成 本）與 贖回 價值的任何差 額
          costs) and the redemption value is recognised in profit or loss over           使用實際利率法於借款期間在損益確
          the period of the borrowings using the effective interest method.              認。

          Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are recognised as            在貸款很有可能部分或全部提取的情
          transaction costs of the loan to the extent that it is probable that           況下，設立貸款融資所支付的費用確
          some or all of the facility will be drawn down. In this case, the fee          認為貸款交易成本，遞延至提取貸款
          is deferred until the draw-down occurs. To the extent there is no              為止。如無法證明該貸款很有可能部
          evidence that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be          分或全部提取，則有關費用作為流動
          drawn down, the fee is capitalised as a pre-payment for liquidity              資金服務的預付款項撥充資本，並於
          services and amortised over the period of the facility to which it             有關融資期間攤銷。
          relates.

          Borrowings are derecognised when the obligation specified in                   當合約規定的責任終止、撤銷或屆滿
          the contract is extinguished, cancelled or expired. The difference             時，借款即終止確認。已償清或轉移
          between the carrying amount of a financial liability that has been             至另一方的金融負債賬面值與已付對
          extinguished or transferred to another party and the consideration             價（包括 已轉讓 非現 金資產或所承 擔
          paid, including any non-cash assets transferred or liabilities                 負債）之 間的差 額於 損益確認為融 資
          assumed, is recognised in profit or loss as finance costs.                     成本。

          Borrowings are classified as current liabilities unless, at the end of         除非在報告期末本集團有權將負債延
          the reporting period, the group has a right to defer settlement of             遲至報告期後至少 12 個月償還，否則
          the liability for at least 12 months after the reporting period.               借款分類為流動負債。

          Covenants that the group is required to comply with, on or before              在將附帶契諾的貸款安排分類為流動
          the end of the reporting period, are considered in classifying                 或非流動時，需要考慮本集團須於報
          loan arrangements with covenants as current or non-current.                    告期末或之前須遵守的契諾。本集團
          Covenants that the group is required to comply with after the                  須於報告期後須遵守的契諾並不影響
          reporting period do not affect the classification at the reporting             於報告日期的分類。
          date.

        differences and to unused tax losses.                                          延稅項資產及負債變動調整。

              and its subsidiaries and associates operate and generate                       報告期末已頒佈或實質頒佈的
              taxable income. Management periodically evaluates                              稅 法 計 算。管 理 層 在 適 用 稅 務
              positions taken in tax returns with respect to situations in                   法規有待詮釋的情況下定期評
              which applicable tax regulation is subject to interpretation                   估 報 稅 表 的 狀 況，並 考 慮 有 否
              and considers whether it is probable that a taxation                           可能稅務機關將接納不確定的
              authority will accept an uncertain tax treatment. The                          稅 務 處 理 方 法。本 集 團 根 據 最
              Group measures its tax balances either based on the most                       接近金額或預期價值計量其稅

              method provides a better prediction of the resolution of the                   案提供更佳預測者而定）。
              uncertainty.

    39.25 Share-based payments                                                     39.25 以股份為基礎的付款
        Share-based compensation benefits are provided to employees                  以股份為基礎的補償福利乃透過購股
        via the share option scheme, share award scheme, and the transfer            權計劃、股份獎勵計劃及盧女士向合
        of shares by Ms. Lu to the eligible participants. Information relating       資格參與者轉讓股份向僱員提供。有
        to these schemes is set out in note 26.                                      關該等計劃的資料載於附註 26。

        Share option scheme                                                          購股權計劃
        The fair value of options granted under the share option scheme is           購股權計劃項下授出的購股權公平值
        recognised as an employee benefits expense with a corresponding              確認為僱員福利開支，同時相應增加
        increase in equity. The total amount to be expensed is determined            權益。將予支銷之總額乃參考授出購
        by reference to the fair value of the options granted:                       股權之公平值釐定：

        •     including any market performance conditions (e.g. the                  •   包括任何市場表現條件（例如實
              entity’s share price)                                                      體的股價）

        •     excluding the impact of any service and non-market                     •   不包括任何服務及非市場表現
              performance vesting conditions (e.g. profitability, sales                  歸屬條件（例如盈利能力、銷售
              growth targets and remaining an employee of the entity                     增長目標及僱員於指定期間內
              over a specified time period), and                                         留任實體）之影響；及

        •     including the impact of any non-vesting conditions (e.g.               •   包括任何非歸屬條件（例如規定
              the requirement for employees to save or hold shares for a                 僱員於一段特定期間保留或持
              specific period of time).                                                  有股份）的影響。

        The total expense is recognised over the vesting period, which               總開支於歸屬期內確認，歸屬期為所
        is the period over which all of the specified vesting conditions             有指定歸屬條件須達成之期間。於各
        are to be satisfied. At the end of each period, the entity revises           個期間結束時，實體根據非市場歸屬
        its estimates of the number of options that are expected to                  及服務條件修訂其估計預期歸屬的購
        vest based on the non-market vesting and service conditions. It              股權數目。實體將對原來估計所作修
        recognises the impact of the revision to original estimates, if any,         訂 而 產 生 的 影 響（如 有）於 損 益 中 確
        in profit or loss, with a corresponding adjustment to equity.                認，並對權益作出相應調整。

        The share option scheme is administered by the Group Employee                購股權計劃乃由集團僱員股份信託管
        Share Trust, which is consolidated in accordance with the                    理，而 該 信 託 已 根 據 附 註 39.1 所 述 原
        principles in note 39.1 When the options are exercised, the trust            則合併。於購股權獲行使時，信託將
        transfers the appropriate number of shares to employee. The                  適當數目的股份轉讓予僱員。所收款
        proceeds received net of any directly attributable transaction costs         項（扣除任何直接應計交易成本）直接
        are credited directly to equity.                                             計入權益。

      39.26 Provisions                                                                 39.26 撥備
           Provisions for legal claims, service warranties and make good                   當本集團因過往事件須承擔現有法律
           obligations are recognised when the group has a present legal or                或推定責任，而解除責任很可能導致
           constructive obligation as a result of past events, it is probable that         資源流出，且金額能可靠估算時，會
           an outflow of resources will be required to settle the obligation               確認法律申索、服務保證及妥善履責
           and the amount can be reliably estimated. Provisions are not                    撥備，但不會就未來經營虧損確認任
           recognised for future operating losses.                                         何撥備。

           Where there are a number of similar obligations, the likelihood                 倘有多項同類責任，則會整體考慮責
           that an outflow will be required in settlement is determined by                 任類別以釐定履行責任時可能流出的
           considering the class of obligations as a whole. A provision is                 資源。即使同一責任類別所涉及任何
           recognised even if the likelihood of an outflow with respect to any             一個項目相關的資源流出可能極低，
           one item included in the same class of obligations may be small.                仍須確認撥備。

           Provisions are measured at the present value of management’s                    撥備按管理層於年末履行當前責任所
           best estimate of the expenditure required to settle the present                 需支出的最佳估計的現值計量。用於
           obligation at the end of the year. The discount rate used to                    釐定現值的貼現率為反映當前市場對
           determine the present value is a pre-tax rate that reflects current             貨幣時間價值及責任特定風險之評估
           market assessments of the time value of money and the risks                     的稅前利率。隨時間流逝而增加的撥
           specific to the liability. The increase in the provision due to the             備確認為利息開支。
           passage of time is recognised as interest expense.

           fair value gains/(losses) on these assets.                                      融資產的利息收入計入該等資產的公
                                                                                           平值收益╱（虧損）淨額。

           Interest income is calculated by applying the effective interest                利息 收入 透過對 金融 資產（惟隨後 發
           rate to the gross carrying amount of a financial asset except                   生信 貸減 值的金 融資 產除外）的賬 面
           for financial assets that subsequently become credit-impaired.                  總值應用實際利率計算。對於信貸減
           For credit-impaired financial assets, the effective interest rate is            值的金融資產，實際利率乃應用於該
           applied to the net carrying amount of the financial asset (after                金融 資產 的賬面 淨值（扣除虧損準 備
           deduction of the loss allowance).                                               後）。

           Interest income is presented as finance income where it is earned               若利息收入來自為現金管理目的而持
           from financial assets that are held for cash management purposes.               有的金融資產，則列為融資收入。任
           Any other interest income is included in ‘Other income’.                        何其他利息收入均計入「其他收入」。

                                                                                       (a)    本集團作為承租人
         (a)   The Group as the lessee
                                                                                              合約可能包含租賃及非租賃組
               Contracts may contain both lease and non-lease
                                                                                              成 部 分。本 集 團 以 租 賃 及 非 租
               components. The Group allocates the consideration in the                       賃組成部分的相對獨立價格為
               contract to the lease and non-lease components based on                        基 準，將 合 約 的 對 價 分 配 至 租
               their relative stand-alone prices. However, for leases of real                 賃及非租賃組成部分。然而，就
               estate for which the Group is a lessee, it has elected not                     本集團為承租人的不動產租賃
               to separate lease and non-lease components and instead                         而 言，其 選 擇 不 將 租 賃 及 非 租
               accounts for these as a single lease component.                                賃 組 成 部 分 分 開，而 就 此 入 賬
                                                                                              為單獨租賃部分。


               Lease terms are negotiated on an individual basis and                          租 賃 條 款 按 個 別 基 準 磋 商，並
                                                                                              包 含 多 項 不 同 的 條 款 及 條 件。
               contain a wide range of different terms and conditions. The
                                                                                              除出租人持有的租賃資產擔保
               lease agreements do not impose any covenants other than
                                                                                              權 益 外，租 賃 協 議 並 無 施 加 任
               the security interests in the leased assets that are held by
                                                                                              何 契 約。租 賃 資 產 不 可 作 為 借
               the lessor. Leased assets may not be used as security for                      款用途的抵押品。
               borrowing purposes.

               The lease payments are discounted using the interest                           租賃付款使用租賃所隱含的利
               rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily                     率貼現。倘無法釐定該利率，則
               determined, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is                         使 用 承 租 人 增 量 借 款 利 率，即
               used, being the rate that the individual lessee would have                     個別承租人為在類似經濟環境
               to pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset of                     中 以 類 似 條 款、抵 押 及 條 件 獲
               similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar economic                  得類似價值使用權資產所需借
               environment with similar terms, security and conditions.                       入的資金須支付的利率。

               To determine the incremental borrowing rate, the Group:                        為釐定增量貸款利率，本集團：

               •     where possible, uses recent third-party financing                        •   在 可 能 情 況 下，使 用 個 別
                     received by the individual lessee as a starting point,                       承租人最近獲得的第三方
                     adjusted to reflect changes in financing conditions                          融資為出發點作出調整以
                     since third party financing was received                                     反映自獲得第三方融資以
                                                                                                  來融資條件的變動

               •     uses a build-up approach that starts with a risk-free                    •    使 用 累 加 法，首 先 就 本 集
                     interest rate adjusted for credit risk for leases held by                     團所持有租賃的信用風險
                     the Group, which does not have recent third party                            （最近並無第三方融資）調
                     financing, and                                                                整無風險利率；及

               •     makes adjustments specific to the lease, e.g. term,                      •   進 行 特 定 於 租 約 的 調 整，
                     country, currency and security                                               例 如 期 限、國 家、貨 幣 及

        (c)   Sublease                                                                (c)   分租
              In classifying a sublease, an intermediate lessor shall classify              就 分 租 的 分 類 而 言，中 介 出 租

              •     if the head lease is a short-term lease that the entity,                •   倘主租賃乃短期租賃而該
                    as a lessee, the sublease shall be classified as an                         實 體 為 承 租 人，則 分 租 應
                    operating lease                                                             分類為經營租賃

              •     otherwise, the sublease shall be classified by reference                •   否 則，分 租 應 參 照 因 主 租
                    to the right-of-use asset arising from the head lease,                      賃產生的使用權資產分
                    rather than by reference to the underlying asset (for                       類，而非參照相關資產（例
                    example, the item of property, plant or equipment                           如 屬 租 賃 標 的 之 物 業、廠
                    that is the subject of the lease).                                          房或設備的項目）分類。

    39.31 Dividend distribution                                                    39.31 股息分派
        Provision is made for the amount of any dividend declared, being              就於年末或之前已宣派，經適當授權
        appropriately authorised and no longer at the discretion of the               且不再由實體自行決定但於年末未分
        entity, on or before the end of the year but not distributed at the           派的任何股息款額計提撥備。
        end of the year.

      39.32 Financial guarantee contracts                                               39.32 財務擔保合約
          Financial guarantee contracts are recognised as a financial                      財務擔保合約於發出擔保時確認為金
          liability at the time the guarantee is issued. The liability is initially        融負債。負債初步按公平值計量，其
          measured at fair value and subsequently at the higher of                         後按以下之較高者計量：

          •     the amount determined in accordance with the expected                      •   根據香港財務報告準則第 9 號「金
                credit loss model under HKFRS 9, ‘Financial Instruments’ and                   融工具」之預期信用虧損模型釐
                                                                                               定的金額及

          •     the amount initially recognised less, where appropriate, the               •   初 步 確 認 金 額 減（如 適 用）根 據
                cumulative amount of income recognised in accordance                           香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則 第 15 號 與 客
                with the principles of HKFRS 15 Revenue from Contracts                         戶的合約收益所確認累計收入
                with Customers.                                                                金額。

          The fair value of financial guarantees on initial recognition equals             財務擔保於初始確認時的公平值等於
          the present value of the premium in an arm’s length transaction.                 公平交易中保證金的現值。

          Where guarantees in relation to loans or other payables of                       倘聯營公司的貸款或其他應付款項擔
          associates are provided for no compensation, the fair values are                 保為無償提供，相關公平值作為注資
          accounted for as contributions and recognised as part of the cost                入賬，確認為投資成本一部分。
          of the investment.
